      u = b, t(u);
    } catch (p) {
      v === i && (s = null, n({ atlasError: p.message }));
    } finally {
      v === i && n({ atlasLoading: !1 });
    }
  }
  return Object.freeze({
    setSource(d) {
      if (l = d, l) return c();
      i++, s = null, t(null), u?.close(), u = null, n({ atlasLoading: !1, atlasError: "", season: r });
    },
    setSeason(d) {
      if (!["spring", "summer", "autumn", "winter"].includes(d)) throw new Error("季節無效");
      return r = d, c();
    },
    retry: c
  });
}
function eR({ api: e, work: t, publish: n, confirm: o, gameId: a = null }) {
  let l;
  const r = Z6({ publish: n, gameId: a ?? "system-base-map", stage: (i) => l.actions.stage(i) }), s = Q6({ api: e, publish: n, path: a ? "/api/games/" + encodeURIComponent(a) + "/copy/map-atlas" : "/api/admin/base-map/atlas", install: (i) => r.setAtlas(i) });
  return l = G6({
    api: e,
    work: t,
    publish: n,
    confirm: o,
    gameId: a,
    paint: (i) => {
      r.setSource(i), s.setSource(i);
    }
  }), Object.freeze({ ...l, actions: Object.freeze({
    ...l.actions,
    attach: r.attach,
    resize: r.resize,
    pick: r.pick,
    pan: r.pan,
    zoom: r.zoom,
    fit: r.fit,
    locate: r.locate,
    focusDiagnostic: r.focusDiagnostic,
    selectGroup: r.selectGroup,
    layer: r.layer,
    previewMaterial: r.previewMaterial,
    season: s.setSeason,
    retryAtlas: s.retry,
    command: (i, u) => t(() => r.command(i, u))
  }) });
}
function oR({ api: e, work: t, publish: n, confirm: o, gameContext: a, createWorkbench: l = eR }) {
  let r = null, s = null, i = null, u = null, c = !1, d = null, m = null;
  const v = () => !!(r && !r.user.mustChangePassword && ["admin", "author"].includes(r.user.role));
  function g(h) {
    h === i && u || (u?.dispose(), u = null, i = h, c = !1, n({
      gameScoped: !0,
      gameId: h,
      loaded: !1,
      exists: !1,
      version: "",
      locked: !0,
      dirty: !1,
      readRevision: 0,
      selected: null,
      layers: [],
      materials: [],
      waters: [],
      groups: [],
      diagnostics: [],
      serverProblems: [],
      message: h ? "" : "請先在頂部選擇自己的遊戲。"
    }), !(!h || !v()) && (u = l({ api: e, work: t, publish: n, confirm: o, gameId: h }), u.setIdentity(r), u.actions.attach(d, m)));
  }
  const p = Object.fromEntries(["reload", "initialize", "stage", "undo", "save", "formDirty", "resize", "pick", "pan", "zoom", "fit", "locate", "focusDiagnostic", "selectGroup", "layer", "previewMaterial", "season", "retryAtlas", "command"].map((h) => [h, (...b) => u?.actions[h](...b)]));
  return p.attach = (h, b) => {
    d = h, m = b, u?.actions.attach(d, m);
  }, p.enter = () => {
    if (!(c || !u))
      return c = !0, u.actions.enter();
  }, Object.freeze({
    actions: Object.freeze(p),
    adoptGame: g,
    canAdoptGame: () => v(),
    selectedGame: () => v() ? i : null,
    canLeave: (h) => u?.canLeave(h) ?? !0,
    dirty: () => u?.dirty() ?? !1,
    render: () => u?.render(),
    setIdentity(h) {
      if (r === h && s === (h?.version ?? null)) {
        u?.setIdentity(h);
        return;
      }
      u?.dispose(), u = null, i = null, r = h, s = h?.version ?? null, g(v() ? a.current() : null), u?.setIdentity(h);
    },
    dispose() {
      u?.dispose(), u = null, d = null, m = null;
    }
  });
}
